Supplemental Direct Testimony of Theodore Poe, Jr. 
Page 1 of 7 
 
Q. 
Please state your name and business address. 
1 
A. 
My name is Theodore Poe, Jr.  My business address is 40 Sylvan Road, 
2 
Waltham, Massachusetts 02451.   
3 
 
4 
Q. 
Are you the same Theodore Poe, Jr. who previously submitted direct 
5 
and supplemental testimony on behalf of KEDNY and KEDLI in these 
6 
proceedings? 
7 
A. 
Yes, I am.  The terms defined in my direct testimony have the same 
8 
meanings here.   
9 
 
10 
Q. 
What is the purpose of your corrections and updates testimony? 
11 
A. 
The purpose of my corrections and updates testimony is to update the 
12 
Companies’ forecasts of projected gas sales for the Rate Year and Data 
13 
Years based on actual data through February 2019. 
14 
 
15 
Q. 
Does your testimony also address the Companies’ June 11, 2019 
16 
supplemental filing?   
17 
A. 
No.  The supplemental filing was made to illustrate the potential impacts to 
18 
the April 30, 2019 filing if KEDNY and KEDLI are unable to obtain 
19 
incremental natural gas supplies from the NESE project.  At this time, the 
20 
Companies remain optimistic that the NESE project will be approved.  
21 


--- Page 3 ---
Page 2 of 7 
 
Therefore, all corrections and/or updates discussed herein pertain to the 
1 
April 30 base rate filing.  The Companies will continue to update the parties 
2 
on the status of the NESE project and implications, if any, to this rate filing.   
3 
 
4

13 
Q. 
Please describe the changes to the Companies’ gas sales forecasts. 
14 
A. 
My direct testimony and exhibits provided forecasts of the Companies’ gas 
15 
sales for the Rate Year and Data Years using actual data through February 
16 
2018.  The Companies’ recently received actual data through February 2019 
17 
and used that data to refresh the gas sales forecasts for the Rate Year and 
18 
Data Years.  Historical data, as presented in my corrections and updates 
19 
exhibits, is now through February 2019.  The forecast data in my corrections 
20 
and updates exhibits now begins in March 2019. 
21 


--- Page 8 ---
Page 7 of 7 
 
 
1 
For KEDNY, incorporation of data through February 2019 results in an 
2 
increase in the Rate Year gas sales forecast from 1,658 million therms to 
3 
1,689 million therms and an increase from 1,271,106 customers to 
4 
1,274,563 customers (as measured in January of the Rate Year).  For 
5 
KEDLI, incorporation of data through February 2019 results in a slight 
6 
decrease in the Rate Year gas sales forecast from 1,075 million therms to 
7 
1,059 million therms with an increase from 610,304 customers to 613,552 
8 
customers (as measured in January of the Rate Year).  While there has been 
9 
little change in the economic forecast between the two gas sales forecasts, 
10 
the inclusion of the additional year of customer data is the principal driver 
11 
of these changes.  The impacts of the increase in the Companies’ gas sales 
12 
forecasts have been reflected in the corrections and updates testimonies of 
13 
Elizabeth D. Arangio, the Rate Design Panels, and the Revenue 
14 
Requirements Panels. 
15 
 
16 
Q. 
Does this conclude your corrections and updates testimony? 
17 
A. 
Yes. 
18
